## Dissent triage — findings not acted on

- chunk a C-007's alternative ("default `companion_voice` to false"): rejected — two voices is the sprint's purpose (PRD FR-2.1); the fix is to plan only viable routes and to say what the default costs in the upgrade note.
- chunk c C-003's "tag `findings[].voice` with the succeeded id": partially — `voice` stays the outer hop so it matches `final_model` and `model_attempts`; the succeeded id is recorded once as `companion_voice.primary_succeeded_model` and in `voices_succeeded_ids`, and drives `independent`.
- chunk c C-006's "import the constants from cheval.py into the stub": not done — the bats stub has no python import path; CMP-17 pins the mapping to the table in `cheval.py` by value and the doc names each code.
- second run, chunk a, primary DISS-001 (BLOCKING): "`capture("^(?<s>.*?[.!?])(\s|$)").s // .` throws when the description has no sentence terminator" — refuted. jq's `capture` yields no output on a non-match (it is `match` under the hood), so `.s // .` keeps the whole description; verified on this host's jq 1.7 with `"no terminator here at all"` and with `""`, and NRM-2 covers the first-sentence path. Not counted.
- eighth run, chunk a2, primary DISS-001 (BLOCKING): "`LOA_ADVERSARIAL_KEEP_WORKDIR=1` should also keep the companion process running" — refuted. The flag keeps FILES for debugging; a background tree that outlives the run was round 1's very first finding (C-001), and the companion's partial results are in the kept workdir. The comment now says so. Not counted.
- fourth run, chunk c C-006 ("restore exit 2 for usage errors"): not done — the header's contract has said exit 1 for usage errors all along, both consumers (`golden-path.sh`, `spiral-evidence.sh`) treat every non-zero exit as not approved, `--json` distinguishes `usage_error`, and the CHANGELOG upgrade note names the change; exit 2 would send a mistyped path back through the legacy pass.
- fourth run, chunk c C-007, second half ("derive CMP-16's workdir probe from `${TMPDIR:-/tmp}`"): not done — the script builds its workdir as a literal `/tmp/adversarial-<sprint>-<pid>` (not `mktemp`), so the probe already matches the only path it can take; noted in the test.
- third run, chunk a, primary DISS-001 (BLOCKING): the same `capture` claim again ("`_normalize_finding_for_validation` returns an empty candidate when the first 200 chars hold no sentence end") — refuted by running the real `_derive_failure_mode` on a 260-character description without terminators (and on a short one): both return JSON with `failure_mode` = the description's first 200 characters, `failure_mode_derived: true`. Not counted; the primary voice repeats it because it reads the diff without executing it.
- third run, chunk c C-002, second half ("a distinct `metadata.status` such as `degraded`"): not done — `reviewed` + `degraded: true` + `status_note` + `primary_voice` is the shape the COMPLETED gate and the degraded-on-rejection path already consume; a new status value would need every consumer taught; the verdict-quality pins cover the silent-consensus risk.
- fifteenth run, chunk a2 primary DISS-001 (BLOCKING): "the skipped-hop list is passed with `--argjson`" — refuted: it is passed with `--arg` and split in jq; the envelope is built on every run of the suites. Not counted.
- fifteenth run, chunk a3 primary DISS-001 (BLOCKING): "the synthetic FAILED envelope is never recorded in `companion.vq`, so aggregation misses the failed companion" — refuted: main feeds the aggregator the synthetic envelope itself (the `elif` on `vq-companion-synthetic.json`), and CMP-27 / CMP-41 pin a failed companion as a dropped voice. Not counted.
- fifteenth run, chunk a4 C-005 ("the refused run's EXIT trap may release the live run's lock"): refuted again — the release acts only on the directory this process took and, since 1p, only when the owner is this `BASHPID`; CMP-34 pins the holder's lock surviving a refused run. Not counted.
- fourteenth run, chunk d primary DISS-001 (BLOCKING): "`_HEADLESS_TIMEOUT_CEILING`'s annotated default sits before a non-default field in a `@dataclass`, so the module fails to import" — refuted: the `@dataclass` decorates `CLIInvocation` (the class above); `HeadlessCLIAdapter` is a plain class, the attribute predates this sprint, and the adapter suite imports the module 2,419 times over. Not counted.
- thirteenth run, chunk a3 C-006, first half ("the lock home under the sprint directory may not exist for a fresh sprint"): refuted — the run lock lives under the per-user lock directory (`_adv_cli_lock_dir`), never in the a2a directory; every companion-suite case is a fresh-sprint run.
- twelfth run, chunk a2 C-004 (main claim: "a malformed / api_failure companion hop leaves rejected rows the fold never summarises"): refuted by the code — a hop that never parsed never reaches the per-finding loop and writes no rows, an all-rejected hop is `reviewed` (degraded later) and IS the final hop whose result the fold reads; only the `-s` hardening above was taken. Not counted.
- twelfth run, chunk a2 C-005 (main claim: "main feeds the companion's own envelopes and the synthetic one together, INV-5 fails"): refuted — main feeds the aggregator EITHER the companion's attempt envelopes (a completed voice) OR the synthetic FAILED one, never both; only the prefix normalisation of the duplicate guard was taken. Not counted.
- twelfth run, chunk a3 C-004, second half ("the cleanup may release a lock it never took"): refuted — `_adv_release_run_lock` acts only on `_ADV_RUN_LOCK_DIR`, which `_adv_take_run_lock` sets after a successful `mkdir`; a refused run never sets it.
- twelfth run, chunk c2 C-004 ("fixture 03 says the calibration exit stores raw `str(exc)` under `message_redacted`"): refuted — `_calibration_needed_exit` passes the exception text through `sanitize_provider_error_message` before it is recorded or printed (cheval.py, the defence-in-depth comment says so); the fixture is a sprint-247 dissent payload describing the pre-fix state. Not counted.
- eleventh run, chunk a2 primary DISS-001, first half ("per-run unique sidecar names by default"): not done — the untagged names are the documented single-writer default that every consumer (the COMPLETED gate, verdict-derive's envelope lookup, the a2a record) reads; the run lock refuses the interleaving instead, and the tag remains the parallel path.
- tenth run, chunk c2 C-002, third suggestion ("prefer `run_tag` content ownership over mtime ordering"): not needed — after b C-001 the mtime decides nothing (an unlisted non-empty sidecar is a violation at any age; the mtime only chooses between the "stale envelope" and "never folded" wording), and ownership is already by name: each run's files are `metadata.rejected_sidecars`.
- tenth run, chunk d C-001, second half ("reduce `_compute_timeout` to a bare isinstance check"): partially — the adapter keeps a finite/positive guard so a bare `ModelConfig` built outside the loader (the tests do this) is bounded the same way, but it no longer warns; the loader is the one reporter.
- second run, chunk c C-003, second half ("keep `voices_planned: 2` and record the duplicate under a distinct voice id, or name the collapse in `chain_health`"): not done — a synthetic id would pollute cost-per-voice analysis and the aggregator's INV-5 forbids one id both succeeded and dropped; `verdict_quality` counts distinct voices, `companion_voice.counted_as` / `independent` / the stderr line are the record, and the resource says so.
- sixteenth run, chunk a3 primary DISS-001 (BLOCKING): "a failing diagnostic substitution in the fold aborts main under errexit" — refuted: the fold runs inside `_folded=$(…) || _folded=""`, where errexit is off for the substitution; the two diagnostic substitutions carry `|| true` anyway so a future call site cannot change that. Not counted.
- sixteenth run, chunk c2c C-001 / c2e C-001, second halves ("restore exit 2 for usage errors", "grep-lock every caller of verdict-derive.sh"): not done — as decided in the fourth run (c C-006): both consumers treat every non-zero exit as not approved, `--json` distinguishes `usage_error`, and exit 2 would send a mistyped path back through the legacy pass; the message assertions and the report's stale wording were taken.
- sixteenth run, chunk c2d C-003, option B ("pin how an unlisted sidecar-named directory is treated"): option A taken — the directory is removed before the positive half and the violation count is pinned; the listed-directory case in the same test already pins `not a regular file`.
- seventeenth run, chunk a1 C-001 ("an accepted repair of the derived field keeps `failure_mode_derived: true`"): unreachable — `validate_finding` rejects a `failure_mode` only when it is empty, and the derivation never yields an empty one (a whitespace-only description derived `" "`; it is now rejected for its description first, and any other description derives its first sentence or head), so the restore only ever re-applies markers to a field the model did not touch. Not counted.
- seventeenth run, chunk c2b primary DISS-001 (BLOCKING): "`_adv_resolve_run_tag` calls `_adv_run_tag`, which the errexit probe does not export" — refuted: the dependency is the other way round (`_adv_run_tag` wraps `_adv_resolve_run_tag`, which calls only `log` and the digest tools the probe stubs); NRM-19 passes on the copy and on the tree. Not counted.
- eighteenth run, chunk b1 primary DISS-001 (BLOCKING): "`has_list` can remain empty after the snapshot read, so sidecar rows beside an envelope without `metadata.rejected_sidecars` are not counted" — refuted: `has_list` is initialised to `false`, the jq snapshot always emits it in the tsv, an empty snapshot returns with its own violation before the read, and the FR-2-marker loop already pins `rejected_summary: []` without `rejected_sidecars` beside two rows as the row-count violation. Not counted.
- eighteenth run, chunk a4 C-003 ("guard the four INV-5 exclusion substitutions"): taken as a hardening without a new test — no reachable trigger on jq 1.7 (`join` accepts every value the envelope can carry); the guard mirrors the `_cid_x` pattern the same passes already use.
- nineteenth run, chunk a2 primary DISS-001 (BLOCKING): "`repair_metadata_json` passes `rhs` with `--argjson` although the value is a shell-produced plain string, often empty" — refuted: `rhs` is passed with `--arg` and split in jq (`if $rhs == "" then [] else ($rhs | split(" ") | unique) end`); the envelope is built on every run of the suites, with and without a skip file (NRM-22). Not counted.
- nineteenth run, chunk a3 C-003 ("the fold never consults the companion envelope's own `metadata.degraded`"): refuted — `degraded` is set only on an audit API failure (a review envelope carries `degraded: false` on every path); a rejection records a degraded VERDICT in the verdict-quality sidecar and the `rejected_summary` row that `verdict-derive.sh` holds the feedback file to, and the fold already unions the rejection counts and summaries of both voices — a companion result never carries a `degraded` the fold could miss. Not counted.
- nineteenth run, chunk c2c C-002 ("the retarget removes the only exit-code distinction between 'the gate could not run' and 'the gate ran and found the file inconsistent'"): not done, as in the fourth and sixteenth runs — now with both consumers audited: `golden-path.sh`'s `_gp_verdict_gate` denies on any non-zero exit and `spiral-evidence.sh` branches on `_vd_rc -eq 0` only, so neither distinguishes 1 from 2; the header's contract says 1, and `--json` carries `usage_error: true` for a caller that needs the difference.
- nineteenth run, chunk a4 C-003 ("initialise `api_exit` / `raw_response` / `vq_sidecar` before the walk"): taken as a hardening without a new test — unreachable while the companion family comes from the configured primary (an all-ceded chain never reads them).

- twentieth run, chunk a1 C-003 ("the skip-row temp file leaks when no workdir is set"): refuted — the file is removed after the metadata is read, on the one exit path the function has (no early return between creation and removal). Not counted.
- twentieth run, chunk a3 C-003, second half ("`ADV_REPAIR_MAX_PER_RUN` may be unset or non-numeric"): refuted — it is `readonly 5` at load; the post budget wraps it in `_adv_num_or` anyway with the hop charge. The first half was taken (above).
- twentieth run, chunk a4 C-002 ("tab is a whitespace IFS character, so an empty middle field shifts the reason into the token"): refuted — every `printf` in `_adv_shared_hop_verdict` emits a non-empty token in each field. Not counted.
- twentieth run, chunk b1 C-004 ("the sidecar glob re-counts moved-aside files"): refuted — the moved-aside name is `<sidecar>.jsonl.prev`, which `adversarial-rejected-<gate>*.jsonl` does not match; verdict-derive.bats s25 pins a `.prev` sidecar uncounted. Not counted.
- twentieth run, chunk c2b primary DISS-001 (BLOCKING): "NRM-26 assumes `XDG_RUNTIME_DIR` is set" — refuted: the suite's setup exports `XDG_RUNTIME_DIR="$TEST_DIR"` for every case (the round-1q isolation). Not counted.
- twentieth run, chunk c2e C-002 ("the load-time report gate never dedupes NaN, and True/1 collide"): refuted — the gate is keyed on `(where, repr(value))`: `repr(float('nan'))` is `'nan'` every time and `repr(True)` differs from `repr(1)`. Not counted.
- twentieth run, chunk d primary DISS-001 (BLOCKING): the `_HEADLESS_TIMEOUT_CEILING` dataclass claim a third time — refuted as in the fourteenth run (`HeadlessCLIAdapter` is a plain class; the suite imports the module on every case). Not counted.

- twenty-first run, chunk a4 primary DISS-001 (BLOCKING): "`_ADV_PREV_FILES` is unset when the reject sidecar is disabled" — refuted: it is initialised at top level at load (`_ADV_PREV_FILES=""`, beside `_adv_prev_files_drop`), and `${v:+…}` never trips `set -u` on an unset variable. Not counted.
- twenty-first run, chunk d primary DISS-001 (BLOCKING): the `_HEADLESS_TIMEOUT_CEILING` dataclass claim a fourth time — refuted as before (`@dataclass` decorates `CLIInvocation`; `HeadlessCLIAdapter` is a plain class). Not counted.
- twenty-first run, chunk c2a C-001 ("`CLAUDE_API_KEY` is an Anthropic alias the setup does not unset"): refuted as stated — `_adv_cred_aliases` lists only `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` for anthropic, and NRM-6/NRM-8 pass with `CLAUDE_API_KEY` exported; the real gap it pointed at (`GOOGLE_API_KEY` / `GEMINI_API_KEY` never unset) is closed: setup unsets the script's own alias table, NRM-30.
- twenty-first run, chunk d C-001 ("a zero-arg `_compute_timeout` override breaks"): refuted as stated — no subclass overrides it and `test_headless_shared_contract.py` enforces that; verifying it found the agy/grok call sites (section 25, acted on).
- twenty-first run, chunk d C-002 ("a `*-headless` provider without `type:` loses its bound"): refuted — a provider with no `type:` loads as `openai`, which is never a headless adapter, so the gate and the dispatch agree; pinned in `test_headless_timeout_seconds_is_cli_only`. Not counted.
- twenty-first run, chunk c1b C-002 ("CMP-22/CMP-26 read the shipped catalog"): kept by design — those two cases pin the shipped catalog's arithmetic on purpose; CMP-31/CMP-33 cover the functions on fixture catalogs. Not counted.

- twenty-second run, chunk a1 C-002 ("a stale rc file feeds the next hop"): refuted — `: > "$_rcf"` truncates it before every hop. Not counted.
- twenty-second run, chunk a1 C-004 ("the repair skip file leaks on an early return"): refuted — it is removed at the end of `process_findings`, and no `return` sits between its creation and that line. Not counted.
- twenty-second run, chunk a2 C-003 ("`_adv_repair_note_secs` / `_adv_repair_retire_hop` can fail under errexit"): refuted — both are variable assignments plus a log line; no file, nothing that returns non-zero. Not counted.
- twenty-second run, chunk a3 C-001 ("the shared hop should leave the companion chain"): kept by design — a shared hop stays (the CHANGELOG upgrade note), independence is judged from answering ids, and the primary's own attempt on it may fail. Not counted.
- twenty-second run, chunk a4 C-003 ("`_companion_deadline_why` can abort the caller"): refuted — it always ends `return 0`. Not counted.
- twenty-second run, chunk a4 C-005 ("keep the companion's boundary result for the primary"): refuted — the primary needs its own answer on the hop; a companion result kept at the boundary is folded and judged by its answering id. Not counted.
- twenty-second run, chunk c2c C-001 ("s14's root-level fixtures leak into later cases"): refuted — `TEST_TMPDIR` is `…-$$`, per test (each bats test runs in its own process), and teardown removes it. Not counted.
- twenty-second run, chunk d C-002, first half ("a zero-arg `_compute_timeout` override raises `TypeError`"): refuted again — no adapter overrides it (grep), and agy/grok pass `model_config`; the second half was acted on (section 26).

- twenty-third run, chunk c2a primary DISS-001 (BLOCKING): "bats fails to parse NRM-15" — refuted: the round's regression ran it (`ok … NRM-15`, the normalise suite 30/30 on the round-1w tree); the quoting defect it brushed is c2a C-002 (acted on). Not counted.
- twenty-third run, chunk a3 C-003 ("`_adv_inv5_rewrite` mis-handles an envelope that planned zero voices"): refuted — both callers route a zero-succeeded envelope to exclusion first (`voices_succeeded_ids | length == 0` → `_excluded_*`), so the rewrite never sees one. Not counted.
- twenty-third run, chunk c1c C-002, CMP-67 half ("CMP-67 needs flock too"): refuted — green with `_ADV_FLOCK_BIN=/nonexistent` injected in the test (on a copy); the CMP-63 half was acted on.
- twenty-third run, chunk c2a C-003, second half ("NRM-10's static count is weak against a split literal"): refuted as a gap — the behavioural leg takes the CLI hop's lock under the test's `XDG_RUNTIME_DIR`, and CMP-121 pins every suite's isolation; the brittleness half was acted on.

- twenty-fourth run, chunk a1 C-001, seq half ("a seq-shaped `companion_chain` is partially honoured"): refuted — yq v4.44.1 errors "cannot index array" on `.companion_chain.anthropic` over a seq, which yields empty and the default chain, as the WARN says; the element half was acted on (section 28).
- twenty-fourth run, chunk a1 C-004 ("the repair skip file falls back to `$TMPDIR` and outlives its pass"): refuted — main always sets `_ADVERSARIAL_WORKDIR` (a `mktemp -d` failure exits 2), and every pass removes its own file at its end with no `return` / `exit` in between; an interrupted pass's file is inside the swept workdir. Not counted.
- twenty-fourth run, chunk a2 primary DISS-001 (BLOCKING): "`fn 9>&-` drops the flock for the function's duration" — refuted: bash saves fd 9 (dup ≥ 10, close-on-exec) while the function runs, so the open file description stays referenced; probed — a lock taken on fd 9 is HELD (`flock -n` fails) inside `probe 9>&-`; the children the function execs do not inherit it, which is the line's purpose. Not counted.
- twenty-fourth run, chunk a2 C-003 ("a failing phase write ends the subshell before the hop"): refuted — every hop call site runs where errexit is ignored (`if repaired=$(…)`, `raw=$(…) || rc=$?`, `( "$@" || exit $? )`), and `_adv_put_state` is best-effort since a3 C-001. Not counted.
- twenty-fourth run, chunk a3 C-002 ("the aggregator holds a succeeded companion sidecar beside the synthetic's dropped entry"): refuted — on the failure branch only the synthetic envelope is aggregated; the companion's own sidecars join `COMPANION_VQ_FILES` on success only. Not counted.
- twenty-fourth run, chunk a4 C-002 ("`_adv_run_interruptible` turns errexit on inside `process_findings`"): refuted — the pre-1x call was `result=$(process_findings …)`, and bash does not carry `-e` into a command substitution (probed `set -e; f(){ false; echo continued; }; r=$(f)` → `continued`), so the semantics are unchanged; the pass's exit status still reaches main through `( "$@" || exit $? )` and `wait`. Not counted.
- twenty-fourth run, chunk b1 primary DISS-001 (BLOCKING): "`jq -Rn … -- file` treats the file as a named argument" — refuted: in jq, `--` ends the options and what follows is an input file (named args need `--args` / `--jsonargs`); probed `jq -Rn '[inputs | select(length>0)] | length' -- two-row.jsonl` → 2, and verdict-derive s13/s16/s19 pin row counts through this path. Not counted.
- twenty-fourth run, chunk d DISS-C-001 ("an HTTP adapter can serve a `kind: cli` model and ignore `headless_timeout_seconds`"): refuted — `_get_adapter_for_entry` routes every `kind: cli` entry to a CLI adapter (`_CLI_ADAPTER_BY_PROVIDER` or the provider's own CLI type, else `ConfigError`), so the gate's disjunction matches the only reader of the field. Not counted.

- twenty-fifth run, chunk a1 C-001 ("`$idx | tonumber` can drop a candidate silently"): refuted — both callers pass the loop counter, and an empty candidate fails `validate_finding` and takes the rejection path. Not counted.
- twenty-fifth run, chunk a1 C-002 ("a blank `id` is kept"): refuted — "", "   " and "\t" fail the safe-token test (probed) and are renumbered with `id_derived`, on both branches. Not counted.
- twenty-fifth run, chunk a2 C-002 ("`_adv_repair_note_secs` / `_adv_repair_retire_hop` can fail under errexit"): refuted again (as the twenty-second run) — in-memory assignments plus a log line; every call site runs errexit-ignored. Not counted.
- twenty-fifth run, chunk a2 C-003 ("the repair model chain can hold a hop outside the floor's base"): refuted — `_repair_model_chain` filters `_repair_chain_base`, which already holds the pin and the answering voice. Not counted.
- twenty-fifth run, chunk a2 C-004 ("`--record-fallback` accepts any `--type`"): refuted — main rejects any type but review|audit (exit 2) before the function is reached, and it has no other caller. Not counted.
- twenty-fifth run, chunk a3 C-003 ("an empty cap reaches `_companion_deadline_why`"): refuted — main wraps both the knob and the computed cap in `_adv_num_or` with a 630 fallback, and `_adv_cli_hop_bound` has no non-numeric output path. Not counted.
- twenty-fifth run, chunk c1a C-002, CMP-12 half ("a rejected payload must make the verdict non-APPROVED"): refuted — no contract says so (probed: APPROVED, the voice answered); the rejection rule is the DEGRADED record `_emit_rejection_degraded` writes from the folded `rejected_count` plus the mandatory section verdict-derive enforces. The CMP-3 half was acted on.
- twenty-fifth run, chunk c1b C-001, holder half ("a leaked holder can hold the test's lock"): refuted — every holder takes a per-test lock file under `$T` (fresh per test) before the code under test runs. The probe half was acted on.
- twenty-fifth run, chunk c1c DISS-C-001 (companion BLOCKING): "CMP-113 never feeds an AWS-shaped key" — refuted: the file holds the literal `AKIAIOSFODNN7EXAMPLE` (`grep -c` → 1); the companion read the dissent content's REDACTED view (`[REDACTED:aws_key]`). The finding's own mutation (the E-token rule widened to `\b[A-Z0-9]{5,}\b`) turns CMP-113 red. Not counted.
- twenty-fifth run, chunk c2b C-002 ("NRM-22's 610 is cheval's formula"): refuted — 610 is the script's own literal default (`_conf_uint … 610`), and `headless_read_floor` is Python-side and never feeds `_ADV_CLI_HOP_TIMEOUT`. Not counted.
- twenty-sixth run, chunk a1 DISS-001 (primary BLOCKING, "a `capture` non-match drops the finding"): refuted — jq 1.7 yields the whole description (`capture` on a non-match is the empty stream; `// .` keeps the input). Pinned by NRM-41. Not counted.
- twenty-sixth run, chunk a2 DISS-001 (primary BLOCKING, twice, "the skipped-hop string goes through `--argjson`"): refuted — it goes through `--arg rhs` and is split inside jq; normalise tests pin `repair_hops_skipped` from a non-empty skip file. Not counted.
- twenty-sixth run, chunk a3 C-001, leading-dash half: refuted — the `--diff-range` parser requires a word character first, before git runs; CMP-128 pins `--output=x`, `-main...HEAD`. The submodule half was acted on.
- twenty-sixth run, chunk a4 C-004 ("a bare `_companion_deadline_why` call can abort main"): refuted — every branch ends `return 0`, and it communicates only through its `printf -v` outvar. Not counted.
- twenty-sixth run, chunk c1a C-001 ("the alias table should be line-per-alias"; c2a C-001 and c2e C-001 the same): refuted — the table's contract is one space-separated line, read by the production probe with the same `read -a`, and google is consulted by no planner path. NRM-39 now asserts each table is one line. Not counted.
- twenty-sixth run, chunk c1b C-001 ("the run-lock tests need flock"): refuted — the run lock is mkdir + a pid token; flock serialises only the takeover. Probed: CMP-34 and CMP-56 pass with `_ADV_FLOCK_BIN=/nonexistent/flock` set inside each body. Not counted.
- twenty-sixth run, chunk c1c C-002 ("CMP-113 lacks an AWS-shaped key"): refuted — the file holds the literal; the companion read the dissent content's redacted view (as the twenty-fifth run's c1c). Not counted.
- twenty-sixth run, chunk c2e C-002 ("a second rootless conftest would evict this one"): refuted — `.claude/adapters/tests/__init__.py` exists, so it registers as `tests.conftest` (probed with `--trace-config`), and it is the only conftest there. Not counted.
- twenty-sixth run, chunk d DISS-001 (primary BLOCKING, "dataclass field ordering in HeadlessCLIAdapter"): refuted — the `@dataclass` decorates `CLIInvocation`; `dataclasses.is_dataclass(HeadlessCLIAdapter)` is False and the adapter suite runs (2449 passed). Not counted.
- twenty-seventh run, chunk a1 DISS-001 (primary BLOCKING, "the `${chain:-$1}` fallback re-adds a filtered answering voice"): refuted — neither filter removes the answering voice (`_adv_repair_hop_shared_now` returns 1 for it first, and the dead-hop filter skips it), and the base chain always ends with `$1`, so the filtered chain is empty only when `$1` is. Not counted.
- twenty-seventh run, chunk a1 C-002 ("the comparison is blind to the derivation markers"): refuted — the accept site deletes the reply's markers and gives back only the original's true ones, so the blindness is never a channel. Not counted.
- twenty-seventh run, chunk a2 C-001, "an empty canonical" half: refuted — the substitution is errexit-ignored and the name comes back unchanged. The splice half was acted on. Attempt 1's primary BLOCKING repeated the twenty-sixth run's refuted `--argjson rhs` claim. Not counted.
- twenty-seventh run, chunk b1 C-001 ("jq 1.6 reads the token after `--` as the program"): refuted — in jq 1.6 (`src/main.c` at tag `jq-1.6`) every token after `--` is an input file; it is `jq -- '<program>'` that 1.6 rejects. Both sites put the program before `--`. Not counted.
- twenty-seventh run, chunk b2 C-006 ("a `--record-fallback` record opens the COMPLETED gate"): refuted — `_artefact_valid` requires `metadata.type` and `metadata.model`, and the record names no model. CMP-158 runs the real hook: a fallback record → exit 2, a run's envelope → exit 0. The audit resource's false "checks that this file exists" was corrected. Not counted.
- twenty-seventh run, chunk c1a C-001, c2a C-001 and c2e C-001's first-line half (the one-line alias table, the fourth to sixth reports): refuted as the twenty-sixth run's — NRM-39 pins each table as one line, read by the production probe with the same `read -a`. Not counted.
- twenty-eighth run, chunk a2 C-003 ("an empty `b` in `_companion_wait_cap` is an arithmetic syntax error"): refuted — `(( … ))` reads `b` by name, so an empty value is 0 (probed under `set -euo pipefail`: cap 600). The helper is called only inside `$(…)`, where errexit is cleared, and the call is wrapped in `_adv_num_or` with the 630 fallback. Not counted.
- twenty-eighth run, chunk c1b C-003 ("CMP-53 breaks the suite's python3-free rule"): refuted — the suite has no such rule. `_cfg_edit` (used by dozens of tests) and `_now_ms` are python3. Not counted.
- twenty-eighth run, chunk c1b C-004, the CMP-60 half ("a failed assertion orphans the stub"): refuted — the stub is `loa-cmp30-stubborn-$$`, which teardown's `pkill -KILL -f "loa-cmp(14|30)-[a-z]+-$$"` ends. The CMP-51 half was acted on. Not counted.
- twenty-eighth run, chunk c2e C-002 ("the conftest lookup is empty in a multi-conftest session"): refuted — `.claude/adapters/tests/` is a package, so its conftest is `tests.conftest`, and pytest's `conftest` eviction applies only to non-package conftests. It passed with a second non-package conftest collected before and after it, under `prepend` and `importlib`, and with the repo's own `evals/fixtures/implement-tasks/01/tests`. Not counted.

- twenty-ninth run, chunk a3 attempt 1 primary DISS-001 (BLOCKING, "`_fold_companion` never receives `type`"): refuted — the fold is called only inside `main`, whose `local type` it reads by bash's dynamic scope; CMP-177 pins an audit fold querying `adversarial-audit` (a `local type=""` shadow turns it red). Not counted.
- twenty-ninth run, chunk a2 C-001, the claude-headless half: refuted — the generated map reads `claude-headless=anthropic` (CMP-163), and no vertex/azure provider exists. The alias half was acted on.
- twenty-ninth run, chunk a3 C-002 ("an equal-key sort drops a pid from the KILL loop"): refuted — each live pid re-adds `_adv_tree_pids` of itself on its own turn; CMP-179 passed on the unfixed code with a keep-last sort, and dropping the self-add turns it red. Not counted.
- twenty-ninth run, chunk a4 C-001 ("the synthesized malformed_response envelope lacks `rejected_summary`"): refuted — process_findings' own malformed_response envelopes carry none either, and verdict-derive reads an absent summary as empty (the real envelope → CONSISTENT); CMP-181 pins it. Not counted.
- twenty-ninth run, chunk b2 C-001, the content half: refuted — BEADS-WORKFLOW.md exists with all four steps. The coverage half was acted on.
- twenty-ninth run, chunk b2 C-002 ("gate `--since` on prior findings"): refuted as design — a wrong `--since` needs misuse of the machine-printed run-start value, `metadata.displaced` and verdict-derive's warning surface it, and the gate would fire on every legitimate round. Not counted.
- twenty-ninth run, chunk c1b C-002 ("CMP-61's post-cap leg waits out its budget"): refuted — the global ceiling has already passed, so the verdict is immediate; the leg is pinned under 3 s. Not counted.
- twenty-ninth run, chunk c1c DISS-001 (BLOCKING, "BANG should be q"): refuted — `_ADV_PRIMARY_BANG` is `$!` captured before the fork, so CMP-135's BANG=p with `$!`=q is the fork window; BANG=q would make two legs identical. A comment now says so. Not counted.
- twenty-ninth run, chunk c1c C-002 ("the re-entered reap reads `_ADV_COMPANION_START`"): refuted — it reads only the frozen tree token; dropping its check turns CMP-115 red. Not counted.
- twenty-ninth run, chunk c2a C-001 ("teardown's unwaited kill races a delete"): refuted — the only holder is NRM-23's `sleep 600`, which writes nothing. C-002 ("scope NRM-10's count to one function"): refuted as design — the count over all functions is what catches a second lock-dir resolver. Not counted.

- thirtieth run, chunk a1 DISS-C-001 ("`KEY=$KEY` reads present though python-dotenv interpolates it empty"): refuted — no consumer interpolates (cheval's DotenvProvider takes values raw; env-loader.sh refuses expansion, bug #898). Not counted.
- thirtieth run, chunk a1 DISS-C-002 ("a failed append collapses the accumulator"): refuted in consequence — the pass fails its final pair, the hop is `malformed_response` and the chain falls through; CMP-184 pins it. Not counted.
- thirtieth run, chunk a2 C-001 ("a host provider other than bedrock reads as the family"): refuted — the generated provider values are the vendors and bedrock only; CMP-163 pins the set. Not counted.
- thirtieth run, chunk a2 C-002 ("`${target#*:}` on a bare Bedrock id"): refuted — cheval's `resolve_alias` splits on the first colon too, and every alias and chain entry is provider-prefixed. Not counted.
- thirtieth run, chunk b2 C-001 ("Phase 0 STOPs on a model-less `nothing_to_review` record"): refuted as design — the gate requires `metadata.model` whatever the status; Phase 0 stops earlier with the remedy. Not counted.
- thirtieth run, chunk c1a DISS-C-001 ("CMP-22 pins the live catalog"): refuted — the catalog is System Zone; a framework change to it is what the leg surfaces. Not counted.
- thirtieth run, chunk c1b DISS-C-001 ("CMP-67 needs flock"): refuted, probed without flock — the takeover runs unserialised and CMP-67 passes. Not counted.
- thirtieth run, chunk c2a DISS-C-001 ("a multi-line alias table leaves aliases unscrubbed"): refuted — the table is one line by contract and the probe reads it the same way. Not counted.
- thirtieth run, chunk e1 DISS-C-002 ("the env strip is a denylist"): refuted — cheval reads one Bedrock routing signal (`loader.py:744`), and it is stripped. Not counted.
- thirtieth run, chunk e2b DISS-C-004 ("the review skills grant br sync only"): refuted — at `7f84c87a` both grant `br comments add *` / `br label add *`; CMP-188 enforces every prescribed step. Not counted.
- thirtieth run, chunk e3 DISS-C-002 ("a test XDG_RUNTIME_DIR falls back to the per-user /tmp lock"): refuted — `_adv_cli_lock_dir` uses the base directly, with no mode check; the lock-dir checks route to unguarded, never to /tmp. Not counted.
- thirty-first run, chunk a1 primary DISS-001 (BLOCKING, "`jq -rn '[inputs]'` reads raw strings"): refuted — `-r` is raw output, not `-R`; a probe on a mixed list keeps only the hop names; CMP-13/160/173 pin it. Not counted.
- thirty-first run, chunk a2 attempt 1 primary DISS-001 (BLOCKING, "`_adv_resolve_run_tag` warns on stdout"): refuted — `log()` writes to stderr (adversarial-review.sh:125); stdout carries only the tag. Not counted.
- thirty-first run, chunk a2 attempt 2 primary DISS-001 (BLOCKING, "`rhs` passed with `--argjson`"): refuted — the call is `--arg rhs`, a repeat of the twenty-sixth and twenty-seventh runs' refuted finding. Not counted.
- thirty-first run, chunk a2 DISS-C-001 ("a headless provider key reads as the family"): refuted — the generated MODEL_PROVIDERS give the vendor for every headless alias; CMP-163 pins the set. Not counted.
- thirty-first run, chunk a3 DISS-C-001 ("the 400-row MODELINV tail misses the companion's row"): refuted on evidence — the busiest 910 s window in this host's ledger holds 30 rows, 13× under the tail, and a miss only costs the diagnostic. Not counted.
- thirty-first run, chunk a3 DISS-C-002 ("the reaper discards an answer written between status and done"): refuted as design — the gap opens only once the post budget has expired, where a timeout is the budget's contract. Not counted.
- thirty-first run, chunk b1 primary DISS-001 (BLOCKING, "`jq -Rn … -- "$f"` reads the filename as an argument"): refuted — without `--args` a positional is an input file; a probe on a two-row sidecar counts 2; the verdict-derive sidecar tests pin it. Not counted.
- thirty-first run, chunk c1a DISS-C-001 ("CMP-14's 5 s cap can fire before the stub spawns"): refuted on evidence — green in every regression of rounds 1a–1af on this host under the KF-037 load, and its red message names the cause. Not counted.
- thirty-first run, chunk c1b DISS-C-002 ("CMP-67 needs `_need_flock`"): refuted again (as the thirtieth run's c1b C-001) — with flock removed from PATH, CMP-67 passes and CMP-63/172 skip. Not counted.
- thirty-first run, chunk c2a DISS-C-001/002/003 (the credential scrub's alias table, eval-in-setup locals, the clock-read regex): refuted — the scrub reads `_adv_cred_aliases` as the probe does; the script has no top-level declare; the regex's false positive is a loud red and no bypass spelling exists in reachable code. Not counted.
- thirty-first run, chunk c2c DISS-C-001 ("an equal-mtime sidecar is classed newer"): refuted — `-nt` is strict (verdict-derive.sh:332). Not counted.
- thirty-first run, chunk c2d DISS-C-002 ("the FIFO watchdog can fire under load"): refuted on evidence — the call takes 0.015–0.036 s under the live dissent's load against a 15 s watchdog. Not counted.
- thirty-first run, chunk c2e primary DISS-001 (BLOCKING, "`_reset_gate_around` is a fixture called directly"): refuted — it is a plain generator (conftest.py:30) the autouse fixture yields from; the test passes. Not counted.
- thirty-first run, chunk d DISS-C-001 ("a long timeout note pushes 'timed out' out of the summary"): refuted — `_adv_error_summary` extracts `timed out after NNNs` first, before the capped note; probed with a 3,000-character note. Not counted.
- thirty-first run, chunk e2a DISS-C-003 ("`no_route` degrades every single-family audit by default"): refuted as design — documented in the twenty-third and twenty-seventh runs (CMP-159); the upgrade note names the opt-out, and DEGRADED is a marker, not a block. Not counted.
- thirty-first run, chunk e2b DISS-C-002 ("KF-037/KF-039 headline fields are stale"): refuted as design — the ledger is append-only and kf-write-lib has no field edit; corrections are Attempts rows (KF-037's knob row, KF-039's stdin row), and the Reading guides are current. Not counted.
- thirty-first run, chunk e3 DISS-C-003 ("`setup_mock_curl`'s guard aborts a `setup_file` caller"): refuted — both callers call it from `setup()`, and both suites skip as deprecated. Not counted.
- thirty-first run, chunk e3 DISS-C-004 ("a 0755 `BATS_TEST_TMPDIR` fails the lock dir's mode check"): refuted — `_adv_cli_lock_dir` (adversarial-review.sh:2693) uses the base directly; the mode and no-symlink checks apply to the lock dir the script makes. Not counted.

- thirty-second run, chunk a1 primary DISS-001 (BLOCKING, "`jq -rn '[inputs]'` reads raw strings"): refuted — a repeat of the thirty-first run's a1 DISS-001; `-r` is raw output. Not counted.
- thirty-second run, chunk a1 DISS-C-002 ("an empty validate_anchor output poisons the accumulator"): refuted — a repeat of the thirtieth run's a1 DISS-C-002; CMP-184 pins the loud `malformed_response` fall-through (a guarded-append draft turned CMP-184 red and was reverted). Not counted.
- thirty-second run, chunk a2 primary DISS-001 (BLOCKING, "`"$@" 9>&-` drops the flock"): refuted — a repeat of the twenty-fourth run's a2 DISS-001; bash keeps the saved fd 9 while the function runs. Not counted.
- thirty-second run, chunk a2 DISS-C-002 ("only bedrock is a host"): refuted — a repeat of the thirtieth run's a2 C-001; CMP-163. Not counted.
- thirty-second run, chunk a3 DISS-C-002 ("the fold's cost arithmetic over an untyped cost"): refuted — `cres` is process_findings' own output, written with `--argjson cost`, so a non-number never reaches the fold. Not counted.
- thirty-second run, chunk b2 DISS-C-002 / e2a DISS-C-003 ("a single-family host's audit is always DEGRADED"): refuted as design — repeats of the thirty-first, twenty-seventh and twenty-third runs; CMP-159. Not counted.
- thirty-second run, chunk c1b DISS-C-002 ("CMP-62's stopped survivor leaks"): refuted — teardown's first line `pkill -KILL -f "loa-cmp(14|30)-[a-z]+-$$"` ends it on every path. Not counted.
- thirty-second run, chunk c1c DISS-C-001 ("a `-diff` attribute hides hunks"): refuted — a repeat of the thirtieth run's DISS-C-002; only the operator's global attributesFile is pinned away, and the repository's own attributes are its declared policy (CMP-186). Not counted.
- thirty-second run, chunk c2c DISS-C-001 ("an mtime tie flips s12/s13"): refuted — `-nt` is strict and both legs write the sidecar first, so a tie takes the asserted branch. Not counted.
- thirty-second run, chunk d DISS-C-001 ("`_compute_timeout(self, model_config=None)` breaks an override"): refuted — no override exists; every call site passes `model_config`; the hook is private to the in-tree family. Not counted.
- thirty-second run, chunk e2a DISS-C-002 ("budget_cents now permits twice the spend"): refuted as design — FR-2's per-voice contract, stated in the upgrade note and both config comments; `companion_voice: false` restores it. Not counted.
- thirty-second run, chunk e2a DISS-C-004, argv half ("agy keeps its prompt on argv"): refuted — a repeat of the thirty-first run's documented residual (agy has no stdin transport). The cwd half is acted on above. Not counted.
- thirty-second run, chunk e2b DISS-C-001 ("Flatline, BB and the companion share one CLI window"): refuted — the operator directive of 2026-10-01, documented with KF-037/KF-038; a one-voice chunk is never approved here. Not counted.
- thirty-second run, chunk e2b DISS-C-002 ("`.loa.config.yaml` encodes this host"): refuted — it is this repository's operator config; the distributed defaults do not route through it. Not counted.
- thirty-second run, chunk e2b DISS-C-003 ("spiral budgets sized for a Sonnet executor"): not a defect — per-session dollar caps are the operator's spend decision; surfaced to the operator. Not counted.
- thirty-second run, chunk e2b DISS-C-004 ("KF-037's workaround field is stale"): refuted as design — the ledger is append-only; the correction is the 2026-10-03 Attempts row. Not counted.
- thirty-second run, chunk e2b DISS-C-006 ("BB's API-key precondition fires on the anthropic voice"): refuted — the precondition is keyed on the single-model `config.model` (adapters/index.ts:47), not on a multi_model voice. Not counted.
- thirty-second run, chunk e2c DISS-C-001 ("the lib suite asserts a ledger repair outside the chunk"): refuted — the repair is in the same commit; the chunking split them. Not counted.
- thirty-second run, chunk e3 DISS-C-002, second half ("the lint names suites the chunk did not show"): refuted — they are in the tree, and their guards are what the lint asserts. Not counted.
- thirty-third run, chunk a1 DISS-C-003 ("an unparseable chain falls through loudly"): refuted — a repeat of the thirtieth and thirty-second runs' a1 findings; CMP-184 pins the loud malformed_response fall-through by design. Not counted.
- thirty-third run, chunk a2 DISS-001 ("`"$@" 9>&-` releases the flock"): refuted — a third repeat (twenty-fourth run, probed): a simple command's redirection applies to the child only, and the subshell's fd 9 keeps the lock. Not counted.
- thirty-third run, chunk a2 DISS-C-001 ("only bedrock is treated as a host"): refuted — a repeat of the thirtieth and thirty-second runs' a2 findings; claude-headless maps to `anthropic` and CMP-163 pins every generated provider value. Not counted.
- thirty-third run, chunk a3 DISS-C-002 ("`_fold_companion` over a non-numeric cost_usd"): refuted — cres is process_findings' own output, where cost_usd is always a number. Not counted.
- thirty-third run, chunk a4 DISS-C-001 ("an errexit-armed `&&` list"): refuted — none of the three lists ends a function body, and a non-final `&&` member never trips errexit (probed under `set -euo pipefail`). Not counted.
- thirty-third run, chunk a4 DISS-C-002 ("the timed-out reaper keeps result.json without a status"): refuted — the walker writes status before phase `done` in one process, the only `done` writer. Not counted.
- thirty-third run, chunk b2 DISS-C-001 ("`companion_voice: false` avoids the degraded marker"): refuted as design — the explicit opt-out is the operator's documented, visible choice (`planned: false`, `voices_planned 1`); the marker is for an unplanned loss. Not counted.
- thirty-third run, chunk b2 DISS-C-004 ("no mechanical DEGRADED_SECURITY_REVIEW check"): not a defect of this cycle — the marker is a judgment rule of the audit resource; making it mechanical is a new gate, filed as a follow-up bead at cycle end. Not counted.
- thirty-third run, chunk c1a DISS-C-002 ("the companion suite keeps a mktemp fallback"): refuted — that fallback is swept by teardown (`CMP_OWN_TMP`); the e3 lint targets the unswept spelling. Not counted.
- thirty-third run, chunk c2a DISS-C-001 ("the normalise suite keeps a mktemp fallback"): refuted for the code (swept, `NORM_OWN_TMP`); the reviewer.md wording was made precise. Not counted.
- thirty-third run, chunk c2b DISS-C-003 ("NRM-49's 15 s bound is flaky"): refuted — the whole file took 3 s under the live dissent load (5× headroom), and the pinned regression was red at 36 s. Not counted.
- thirty-third run, chunk c2d DISS-C-002, first half ("a TERM before `s=$!`"): refuted — the watchdog is TERMed only after `wait "$reader"` returns; the second half (no KILL escalation) was acted on. Not counted.
- thirty-third run, chunk c2e DISS-C-002 ("the adapters pytest shares the live workspace"): refuted — the autouse `_private_headless_workspace` fixture gives each test its own 0700 XDG root and pins the trusted ancestors. Not counted.
- thirty-third run, chunk e1 DISS-C-006 ("the stable cwd and its refusals are untested"): refuted — `test_claude_runs_in_one_stable_private_cwd` and `test_the_stable_workspace_refuses_a_symlink_or_an_open_directory` cover both. Not counted.
- thirty-third run, chunk e2a DISS-C-001 ("agy's prompt rides argv"): refuted as a defect — already documented in the adapter; agy has no stdin or file transport, is not installed and is disabled, and an ARG_MAX failure walks on. Not counted.
- thirty-third run, chunk e2b DISS-C-002 ("KF-037/KF-039 header fields are stale"): refuted by contract — the ledger header allows only recurrence, attempts and status edits; the 2026-10-03 rows are the correction. Not counted.
- thirty-third run, chunk e3 DISS-C-002 ("`setup_mock_curl` may run without BATS_TEST_TMPDIR"): refuted — both callers call it from setup(), where it is set; no setup_file() caller exists. Not counted.

- thirty-fourth run, chunk a2 DISS-001 ("`repair_metadata_json` binds rhs with --argjson", primary BLOCKING): refuted — the site is `--arg rhs`, a string split in jq; no `--argjson rhs` exists. Not counted.
- thirty-fourth run, chunk a2 DISS-C-001 ("only bedrock treated as a host"): refuted — a fourth repeat; claude-headless maps to `anthropic` and CMP-163 pins every provider value. Not counted.
- thirty-fourth run, chunk a2 DISS-C-002 ("an unguarded `_conf_uint` at load can kill the run"): refuted by probe — `_conf_uint` returns 0 on both paths; a malformed value WARNs and the run continues. Not counted.
- thirty-fourth run, chunk a4 DISS-C-001 ("re-collection tokens glued without a space"): refuted — `_adv_pid_tokens` prints a trailing space per pid and command substitution strips only newlines (probed). Not counted.
- thirty-fourth run, chunk a4 DISS-C-003 ("`_adv_resolve_run_tag` / `_companion_deadline_why` unguarded under errexit"): refuted — both return 0 on every path by construction (probed). Not counted.
- thirty-fourth run, chunk b2 DISS-C-002 ("default true guarantees DEGRADED on a single-family host"): refuted as design — a repeat of the thirty-third run's b2 DISS-C-001; the block ships disabled and names `false` as the opt-out. Not counted.
- thirty-fourth run, chunk c1b DISS-C-002 ("CMP-82/60 leave a stopped TERM-immune sleep"): refuted — teardown's `pkill -KILL` matches the stub's exec name and SIGKILL ends a stopped process. Not counted.
- thirty-fourth run, chunk c1b DISS-C-003 ("CMP-56's final leg needs flock"): refuted by probe — without flock the takeover runs unserialised by design and CMP-56 passes. Not counted.
- thirty-fourth run, chunk c1b DISS-C-005 ("CMP-53's python3 is the one remaining call"): refuted — the premise is false; the suite uses python3 in six places. Not counted.
- thirty-fourth run, chunk c2b DISS-C-002 ("NRM-49's 15 s bound is flaky"): refuted — the whole run measured ~4.7 s under the live dissent; the regression it catches cost 54 s. Not counted.
- thirty-fourth run, chunk c2c DISS-C-001 ("s12/s13 can flip to 'is newer' on a coarse-mtime filesystem"): refuted — `-nt` is strict, a tie takes the asserted branch, and the sidecar is written first. Not counted.
- thirty-fourth run, chunk d DISS-C-003 ("a post-success cleanup OSError reaches the outer handler"): refuted — every teardown ignores errors and the inner try converts the subprocess's own. Not counted.
- thirty-fourth run, chunk e1 DISS-001 ("grok's finally can raise UnboundLocalError", primary BLOCKING): refuted — `workspace` is assigned None before the try and the finally guards it. Not counted.
- thirty-fourth run, chunk e1 DISS-C-001 ("a non-OSError escapes private_workspace_base"): refuted — every failure path raises OSError; the NUL shape cannot arrive through execve. Not counted.
- thirty-fourth run, chunk e1 DISS-C-002 ("agy's per-hop cwd vs folder trust"): refuted as a defect — documented; agy is disabled and its trust prompt fails walkable on the closed stdin. Not counted.
- thirty-fourth run, chunk e1 DISS-C-003 ("agy's prompt readable through /proc"): refuted as new — the accepted, documented exposure of round 1af. Not counted.
- thirty-fourth run, chunk e1b DISS-C-003 ("the workspace suite leaks XDG_RUNTIME_DIR"): refuted — conftest's autouse fixture monkeypatches it per test, so it is restored (probed). Not counted.
- thirty-fourth run, chunk e1b DISS-C-004 ("gemini may not join stdin with -p"): refuted — verified in the installed 0.41.2; older clients are refused by the service (KF-018). Not counted.
- thirty-fourth run, chunk e2a DISS-C-001 ("other adapters' prompt transport unstated"): refuted — every other adapter keeps the prompt off argv; agy is the documented exception. Not counted.
- thirty-fourth run, chunk e2a DISS-C-002 ("an uncreatable run-lock dir proceeds unguarded"): refuted as design — CMP-93. Not counted.
- thirty-fourth run, chunk e2a DISS-C-003 ("budget_cents per voice doubles spend"): refuted as design — a repeat of the thirty-second run's e2a DISS-C-002. Not counted.
- thirty-fourth run, chunk e2a DISS-C-004 ("no_route degrades every single-family audit"): refuted as design — CMP-159. Not counted.
- thirty-fourth run, chunk e2b DISS-C-001 ("KF-037/039 header fields stale"): refuted by contract — append-only ledger; the Reading guides are accurate. Not counted.
- thirty-fourth run, chunk e2b DISS-C-002 ("Fable caps × max_phase_retries ⇒ 4× implement spend"): refuted — max_phase_retries retries the gates, never the implement session. Not counted.
- thirty-fourth run, chunk e2b DISS-C-004 ("skill-invariants 'System none' while the script writes ~/.cache"): refuted — the row's zones are the repo's Three-Zone names. Not counted.
- thirty-fourth run, chunk e3 DISS-C-003 ("BATS_TEST_TMPDIR is 0755"): refuted — hypothetical; the lock code judges only its own 0700 subdirectory, and a tightening would turn tests red, not pass silently. Not counted.

Not counted.
- thirty-fifth run, chunk a2 DISS-C-001 ("`_adv_cli_bin_for`'s yq read aborts callers under errexit"): refuted by probe — every call site is a command substitution, where errexit is off without `inherit_errexit`. Not counted.
- thirty-fifth run, chunk a2 DISS-C-003 ("the lock-timeout marker write exits 1 before `exit 124`"): refuted by probe — every caller that sets the marker runs inside `$(…)` or `if`, where errexit is off; 124 is returned. Not counted.
- thirty-fifth run, chunk c1c DISS-C-003 ("CMP-140 replaces HOLDER_PIDS"): refuted — setup's empty array is the only earlier writer; the trailing reset is deliberate. Not counted.
- thirty-fifth run, chunk c2b DISS-C-002 ("NRM-49/53's 15 s bounds are flaky"): refuted — both tests take about 6.8 s under live-dissent load; the regression they guard took 54 s. Not counted.
- thirty-fifth run, chunk c2e DISS-C-002 ("the credential scrub and probe split aliases differently"): refuted — both read `_adv_cred_aliases` with the same `read -r -a`. Not counted.
- thirty-fifth run, chunk d DISS-C-001 ("cleanup OSError replaces a finished result"): refuted — every workspace is removed with `ignore_errors=True` and `_release_slot` swallows its OSErrors. Not counted.
- thirty-fifth run, chunk d DISS-C-003 ("the stable workspace should delete a project file"): refuted as designed — refusal over deleting the user's file (the thirty-third run's decision); the refusal is the hop's recorded error, and a one-voice chunk is never approved. Not counted.
- thirty-fifth run, chunk e1b DISS-C-001 ("the HOME exemption is claude's only"): refuted — `_project_marker` exempts every project file at HOME; a coverage pin added, mutation-proven. Not counted.
- thirty-fifth run, chunk e2a DISS-C-001 ("other adapters' prompts on argv"): refuted — codex and cursor use stdin, grok `--prompt-file`; agy alone is on argv (bd-ugmi). Not counted.
- thirty-fifth run, chunk e2a DISS-C-003 ("the run lock's infrastructure failure proceeds"): refuted as designed — WARN by the twenty-first run's decision. Not counted.
- thirty-fifth run, chunk e2b DISS-C-001 ("spiral caps not sized for Fable"): refuted as decided — a duplicate of the thirty-third run's e2b DISS-C-003; a spend decision for the operator, now bead bd-xqjw. Not counted.
- thirty-fifth run, chunk e2b DISS-C-002 ("every Claude hop on the CLI adds contention"): refuted as designed — the operator's Fable directive, and the http ids are dead without a key on this host. Not counted.
- thirty-fifth run, chunk e2b DISS-C-003 ("BB fails without ANTHROPIC_API_KEY"): refuted — the precondition keys on `isHeadlessModel(config.model)` (adapters/index.ts:48). Not counted.
- thirty-fifth run, chunk e1 DISS-C-002 ("agy's per-hop cwd may break folder trust"): plausible, unverifiable here (no agy on the host); deferred to the operator re-probe, bead bd-ugmi — agy is disabled. Not counted.
- thirty-fifth run, chunk e3 DISS-C-003, its other half ("setup_mock_curl exits under setup_file"): refuted — both gpt-review callers run it from `setup()` (gpt-review-api.bats:28, gpt-review-request.bats:22). Not counted.

- thirty-sixth run, chunk a4 DISS-C-001 ("a failed fork publishes a stale `$!`"): refuted by probe — bash aborts the top-level command after a failed fork (exit 254). Not counted.
- thirty-sixth run, chunk c2b DISS-C-001 ("NRM-49/53 assert wall clock"): refuted — the bound is the test's point (linear-time cleaning); about 3.2 s and 1.7 s under load against 15 s. Not counted.
- thirty-sixth run, chunk c2e DISS-001 ("the gate-reset test calls the fixture directly"): refuted — `_reset_gate_around` is a plain generator; the fixture is separate. Not counted.
- thirty-sixth run, chunk c2e DISS-C-002 ("the credential scrub reads one line"): refuted — it mirrors the probe's own `read -r -a`. Not counted.

- thirty-sixth run, chunk e2a DISS-C-001 ("without flock every dissent runs with no lock"): refuted — the mkdir run lock is still taken; only a dead holder's takeover is unserialised (CHANGELOG wording corrected, CMP-238). Not counted.
- thirty-sixth run, chunk e2a DISS-C-003 ("codex/cursor/grok may still use argv"): refuted — stdin and `--prompt-file`, pinned by the shared contract (the bullet now states every transport). Not counted.
- thirty-sixth run, chunk e2b DISS-C-001 ("the spend caps were sized for sonnet/opus"): refuted as decided — the operator's spend decision (run 33), retries re-run only gates (run 35). Not counted.
- thirty-sixth run, chunk e2b DISS-C-003 ("rewrite the stale KF-037/KF-039 header fields"): refuted by contract — the append-only ledger, superseding attempt rows. Not counted.
- thirty-sixth run, chunk e3 DISS-C-001 ("XDG_RUNTIME_DIR under BATS_TEST_TMPDIR overstates isolation"): refuted — the comment claims only the CLI lock, which is accurate; no suite reaches a real CLI. Not counted.
- thirty-sixth run, chunk e3 DISS-C-002 ("setup_mock_curl fails under setup_file"): refuted — both callers run it from `setup()`. Not counted.
